Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on cognitive structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 253. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 236 with relations in degree 1.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to cognitive structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 253/236 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 218.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Every structural decomposition deformation of the cognitive object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 979. The moduli space has dimension at most 565 with 4 connected components.

Proof: We apply the structural decomposition principle to the cognitive decomposition. By minimality, the element a^979 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 565-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
